# Agents · P · part 12

1,040 entries, pipeline-monitor to Plan Consistency Reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pipeline-monitor**: Polls a specific GitLab CI pipeline on gitlab.gwdg.de (default: latest on main for ahahn/spectrafit-core) until terminal, then emits a structured report: state per job, durations, and tail of any failing job log. Use when the user pushes a branch and says "tail the pipeline", "watch CI", "what's the pipeline doing", or "monitor the build". DO NOT USE for: classifying the failure (use ci-failure-router); fixing the failure (route to specialist agent). ([Anselmoo/SpectraFit-Core](https://github.com/Anselmoo/SpectraFit-Core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pipeline-monitor.agent.md))

- **piping-flows**: A **piping flow** is the parser's single shape for "a value passed through transformations", however the source spells it. `pipe(x, f, g)`, `x.pipe(f, g)`, `g(f(x))`, `Effect.map(x, h)` and `Effect.fn(body, f)` all become a **subject** followed by ordered **transformations**. Reads the pitwall SKILL.md in full before any work; substituting general-purpose skips that read and drifts.
